### [67] [Deep Learning for Automated Quantification of Tumor-Associated Macrophages from H&E-Stained Slides in Diffuse Large B-Cell Lymphoma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19283)
*Anastasiia Studenikina,Svetlana Illarionova,Joaquim Carreras,Daniil Sulimov,Olga Filimonova,Dmitry Zvezdin,Arsenii Galimov,Ivan Tyukin,Rifat Hamoudi,Maxim Sharaev*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本研究探索利用深度学习从标准HE染色组织切片中直接量化弥漫大B细胞淋巴瘤（DLBCL）中的M2型肿瘤相关巨噬细胞（TAMs），以替代资源密集型的CD163免疫组化（IHC）检测。基于52例患者、1,713个TAM实例的数据集，评估了五种深度学习架构，其中Swin-U-Net在分割精度上表现最佳（Precision 0.694, F1-score 0.633），并与IHC得出的CD163密度阈值（20%）高度吻合，预测结果在生存分析中呈类似趋势，提示其可作为可扩展且经济的IHC替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: M2型肿瘤相关巨噬细胞是DLBCL疾病侵袭性的重要指标，但传统的CD163免疫组化方法资源密集、成本高昂且难以大规模应用。本研究旨在验证深度学习能否直接从常规HE染色切片中量化TAMs，以提供一种可扩展且经济的替代方案。

Method: 使用52例DLBCL患者的高分辨率HE图像和IHC验证注释（1,713个TAM实例），评估五种深度学习架构：U-Net、Swin-U-Net、Cerberus-U-Net3+、YOLOv11和HoVer-Net。通过CD163 TAM密度进行生存分析（KM曲线和多因素Cox回归，校正IPI、分子亚型、EBV状态和年龄），并与IHC结果进行对比验证。

Result: CD163高TAM密度（>20.04%）与总生存率（HR 2.73, p=0.012）和无进展生存率（HR 2.88, p=0.011）显著降低相关。Cerberus-U-Net3+达到最高敏感度（Recall 0.656），Swin-U-Net在分割保真度上最优（Precision 0.694, F1-score 0.633）。基于Swin-U-Net预测的CD163水平生存分析得出20.8%的临界值，与IHC接近，且高预测值患者呈现总生存率下降趋势（HR 2.63, p=0.083）。多因素分析中CD163密度仅呈趋势性意义（OS p=0.062, PFS p=0.120）。

Conclusion: 采用移位窗口自注意力机制的深度学习架构（特别是Swin-U-Net）在TAM定量方面表现出与IHC相近的预后分层能力，可作为DLBCL中TAM预后评估的潜在可扩展且经济的IHC替代方案。

Abstract: While M2-polarized tumor-associated macrophages (TAMs) have been established as indicators of disease aggressiveness in diffuse large B-cell lymphoma (DLBCL), traditional CD163 immunohistochemistry (IHC) remains resource-intensive. This study aims to investigate the feasibility of using deep learning to quantify TAMs directly from standard hematoxylin and eosin-stained (HE) tissue sections. Using a curated dataset of 52 patients with DLBCL, with high-resolution HE images and IHC-validated annotations (1,713 TAM instances), five architectures were evaluated: U-Net, Swin-U-Net, Cerberus-U-Net3+, YOLOv11, and HoVer-Net. High CD163 TAM density (>20.04%) was associated with significantly reduced overall survival (HR 2.73; 95% CI:1.21-6.16; p=0.012) and progression-free survival (HR 2.88; 95% CI: 1.23-6.76; p=0.011). In multivariate Cox proportional hazards analysis adjusting for IPI, molecular subtype (GCB/non-GCB per Hans algorithm), EBV status, and age, CD163 TAM density showed a prognostic trend for overall survival (HR 3.24; 95% CI: 0.94-11.15; p=0.062) and progression-free survival (HR 2.41; 95% CI: 0.80-7.32; p=0.120). Among the evaluated models, the domain-specific Cerberus-U-Net3+ achieved the highest sensitivity (Recall 0.656), while the Transformer-based Swin-U-Net demonstrated superior segmentation fidelity (Precision 0.694, F1-score 0.633). Additionally, the survival analysis based on the predicted Swin-U-Net CD163 level revealed a 20.8% cutoff point for patients with high and low CD163 levels near IHC, as well as a downward trend in overall survival among patients with higher predicted CD163 values of 2.63 (95% CI: 0.85-8.33; p=0.083). These findings suggest that deep learning architectures using shifted-window self-attention show potential as a candidate surrogate for IHC that is scalable and cost-effective for prognostic assessment of TAMs in DLBCL.

</details>


### [68] [MOSAIC: A Self-supervised Dynamic Multi-encoding Reconstruction Framework for 3D Late Gadolinium Enhancement MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19506)
*Muhammad A. Sultan,Yingmin Liu,Katherine Binzel,Katarzyna E. Gil,Karolina M. Zareba,Rizwan Ahmad*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: MOSAIC是一种自监督动态重建框架，用于高度欠采样的双回波三维晚期钆增强（3D LGE）MRI，能直接从欠采样数据中联合建模多回波图像内容、线圈灵敏度图和逐心跳非刚性运动，无需全采样训练数据或预计算灵敏度图，在超过1000倍加速下实现运动分辨的自由呼吸3D LGE成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D LGE MRI在自由呼吸和高度欠采样条件下重建面临困难，现有方法需要全采样训练数据集或准确的预计算灵敏度图，且通常将数据分箱到不同运动状态（含或不含运动补偿），难以实现逐心跳级别的运动分辨成像。

Method: MOSAIC框架采用自监督方式，直接从获取的欠采样数据中联合估计多回波图像内容、线圈灵敏度图以及逐心跳非刚性运动。与将数据分箱到不同运动状态的现有方法不同，MOSAIC从每个心跳重建运动分辨的3D LGE图像。评估使用带有模拟心肌疤痕的数字体模以及体内动物和人体研究。

Result: 体模实验中，MOSAIC在峰值信噪比（PSNR）和结构相似性指数（SSIM）上均优于低秩深度图像先验（LR-DIP）重建及其消融变体。在动物和人体研究中，MOSAIC的盲评专家图像质量评分高于在线图像导航压缩感知（inline image-navigated compressed sensing）和LR-DIP重建。

Conclusion: MOSAIC证明了在超过1000倍加速因子下进行运动分辨自由呼吸双回波3D LGE MRI的可行性，相比最先进的对比方法，在细节保持和伪影抑制方面均有显著提升。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop and evaluate a self-supervised dynamic reconstruction framework for highly undersampled dual-echo three-dimensional late gadolinium enhancement (3D LGE) MRI.
  Methods: MOSAIC jointly models multi-echo image content, coil sensitivity maps, and beat-specific nonrigid motion directly from acquired undersampled data, without requiring fully sampled training datasets or accurate precomputed sensitivity maps. Unlike existing methods that bin the acquired data into different motion states, with or without motion compensation, MOSAIC reconstructs a motion-resolved 3D LGE image from each heartbeat. The method was evaluated using digital phantoms with simulated myocardial scars and in vivo animal and human studies.
  Results: In phantom experiments, MOSAIC achieved higher peak signal-to-noise ratio and structural similarity index measure than low-rank deep image prior reconstruction and ablation variants of MOSAIC. In animal and human studies, MOSAIC achieved higher blinded expert image-quality scores than inline image-navigated compressed-sensing and low-rank deep image prior reconstructions.
  Conclusion: MOSAIC demonstrated the feasibility of motion-resolved free-breathing dual-echo 3D LGE MRI at acceleration factors exceeding 1,000, with improved detail preservation and artifact suppression relative to the state-of-the-art comparison methods.

</details>


### [69] [Loss-Resilient Semantic Communication over Packet-Loss Networks at Extreme-Low Bandwidth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19590)
*Shengshi Yao,Jincheng Dai,Sixian Wang,Guo Lu,Kai Niu,Wenjun Xu,Wenjun Zhang,Ping Zhang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: ResiGLC是一个面向极低带宽丢包网络的损失鲁棒性生成式潜在编码框架，通过语言模型的上下文预测能力和掩码学习策略实现任意上下文建模，并在接收端采用渐进式鲁棒解码管线，联合优化压缩效率与丢包弹性，在丢包条件下显著提升感知保真度和真实感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式语义编解码器仅针对压缩效率进行优化，在面对丢包导致的传输错误时鲁棒性不足。由于生成式潜在表征高度紧凑，丢包造成的损坏会导致保真度和真实感严重退化，且错误在潜在上下文和多步解码过程中严重传播。因此需要设计一个兼具压缩效率和丢包弹性的生成式编码框架。

Method: 提出ResiGLC框架，其核心方法包括：(1) 利用语言模型的内在上下文预测能力，结合掩码学习策略，实现对潜在码的任意上下文建模，从而缓解错误传播并应对不可预测的丢包模式；(2) 在接收端设计渐进式鲁棒解码管线，分别利用潜在码之间的上下文关系以及生成潜在空间中的多模态语义先验；(3) 联合优化压缩效率与丢包弹性，实现动态丢包条件下的优雅降级。

Result: 大量实验表明，在丢包网络条件下，ResiGLC以极低带宽成本有效提升了感知保真度和真实感方面的损失鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ResiGLC证明了生成与压缩之间的目标一致性可以用于构建损失鲁棒的编码框架，通过上下文建模与渐进式解码在极低带宽丢包网络中实现可靠性视觉通信，为生成式语义通信在传输不可靠场景下的应用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: In extreme-low bandwidth network scenarios, generative semantic codecs have emerged as promising solutions to reduce bandwidth cost for visual communications. However, these learned codecs are usually optimized solely for compression efficiency and thus not robust against transmission errors. Corruptions due to packet-loss among these highly compact generative latent representations often cause more critical degradation in fidelity and realism, intensified by the severe error propagation across the latent contexts and multi-step decoding process. In this paper, we propose ResiGLC, a novel loss-resilient generative latent coding framework designed for robust semantic communication over extreme-low bandwidth packet-loss networks. Motivated by the inherent goal-consistency between generation and compression, we sufficiently exploit the impressive in-context predictive capabilities of language models. Integrated with the masked learning strategy, our model supports arbitrary context modeling of latent codes, which could mitigate the error propagation and handle unpredictable packet loss patterns. At the receiver, a progressive resilient decoding pipeline is presented, which leverages both the contextual relationship of the latent codes and the multi-modal semantic prior in the generative latent space, separately. By jointly optimizing toward both compression efficiency and packet-loss resilience, our proposed progressive decoding mechanism offers graceful performance when dealing with dynamic packet losses. Through extensive experimental evaluations, we establish that under packet-loss network conditions, ResiGLC can effectively improve the loss-resilience in terms of perceptual fidelity and realism qualities with extreme-low bandwidth cost.

</details>


### [70] [AsymFeX: A Symmetry-Driven Framework for Ischemic Stroke Segmentation Across Imaging Modalities and Stroke Stages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19769)
*Maunil Shah,Vaanathi Sundaresan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段、兼容nnU-Net的3D分割方法用于急性缺血性卒中（AIS）病灶分割。第一阶段校正头部倾斜以对齐真实解剖学中矢状面；第二阶段采用新颖的非对称特征提取（AsymFeX）模块，通过跨半球注意力、特征差异估计和双尺度门控在局部3x3x3邻域内比较每个体素与其对侧对应体素。在AISD数据集上达到0.6796 Dice、23.53 mm HD95和7.69 mL AVD的优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非对比CT（NCCT）是缺血性梗死诊断的首选影像方式，但梗死区域对比度微弱，手动勾画耗时费力。临床实践中常通过比较左右脑半球来定位梗死灶，作者受此启发，希望利用大脑对称性来提升AIS病灶分割的准确性和效率。

Method: 方法包含两个阶段：第一阶段通过校正头部倾斜，将每个扫描对齐到真实的解剖学中矢状面；第二阶段应用新型AsymFeX模块，在局部3x3x3邻域内通过跨半球注意力机制将每个体素与真实对侧对应体素进行比较，并结合特征差异估计和双尺度门控来同时捕捉大、小梗死灶。整个方法兼容nnU-Net架构。

Result: 在AISD数据集上，该方法取得0.6796 Dice、23.53 mm HD95、7.69 mL AVD的结果，显著优于现有最先进方法；在70 mL溶栓资格阈值上提供临床相关的体积分析。在ATLAS v2.1和ISLES'24上的概念验证表明，相同的对称驱动设计无需架构更改即可跨成像模态和卒中时间点泛化，并辅以不确定性分析评估临床部署下的可靠性。

Conclusion: 基于大脑对称性驱动的跨半球特征比较策略在AIS病灶分割中有效且显著优于现有方法，并能跨模态、跨时间点泛化，具备临床实际部署潜力。代码已公开。

Abstract: Fast and accurate segmentation of Acute Ischemic Stroke (AIS) lesions is essential for stroke prognosis and treatment planning. Non-contrast CT (NCCT), the first-line imaging modality for diagnosing ischemic infarcts, exhibits subtle infarct contrast, making manual delineation slow and labor-intensive. Motivated by this, and by the clinical practice of comparing brain hemispheres to localize infarcts, we propose a two-stage, nnU-Net-compatible 3D segmentation method. The first stage corrects head tilt to align each scan to its true anatomical mid-sagittal plane; the second applies a novel Asymmetric Feature Extraction (AsymFeX) module, comparing each voxel to its true contralateral counterpart within a local 3 x 3 x 3 neighborhood via cross-hemispheric attention, feature disparity estimation, and dual-scale gating to capture both large and small infarcts. On AISD, our method achieves 0.6796 Dice, 23.53 mm HD95, and 7.69 mL AVD, significantly outperforming existing state-of-the-art methods, with clinically relevant volumetric analysis at the 70 mL thrombolysis-eligibility threshold. Proof-of-concept evaluation on ATLAS v2.1 and ISLES'24 demonstrates that the same symmetry-driven design generalizes across imaging modalities and stroke time points without architectural changes, further supported by an uncertainty analysis assessing reliability under clinical deployment. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/biomedia-lab/AIS-detection.

</details>


### [71] [MOSAIC: Modality-agnostic Spectral Alignment for Federated Image-level Weakly Supervised Tumor Segmentation under Client-specific Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19788)
*Tarun Kumar Garg,Vaanathi Sundaresan*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: MOSAIC是首个面向客户端特定缺失模态的模态无关联邦学习框架，用于弱监督二元肿瘤分割。通过客户端特定模态对齐模块、频谱原型对齐损失和联邦细化网络，在仅使用图像级标签的情况下接近全监督精度，在FeTS2022上达到0.84 Dice，并支持动态新客户端加入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床环境中的多模态融合面临不完整和异构的模态子集，且隐私约束禁止集中式数据共享。现有联邦学习虽缓解了数据共享约束，但面临客户端特定缺失模态问题；弱监督与联邦学习的联合应用在异构缺失模态和图像级标签条件下尚未被研究。

Method: 提出MOSAIC框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 客户端特定模态对齐模块，将可用通道融合到共享潜空间，无需模态身份先验；(2) 频谱原型对齐损失，利用紧凑不可逆频域统计量协调跨客户端分布偏移；(3) 联邦细化网络，对CAM伪标签去噪生成精确掩码，打破弱监督精度上限。

Result: 在三个多机构脑肿瘤基准（FeTS2022、BraTS-MEN、BraTS-SSA）上显著优于所有图像、框和点监督基线，仅用图像级标签即接近全监督精度，在FeTS2022上达到0.84 Dice。动态新客户端加入无需重训练，Dice损失仅在0.01-0.04范围内。

Conclusion: MOSAIC有效解决了联邦学习下客户端特定缺失模态与弱监督相结合的问题，验证了模态无关融合与频谱原型对齐在跨客户端分布偏移下的有效性，同时具备良好的可扩展性以支持新机构的动态加入。

Abstract: Trustworthy multimodal fusion in clinical settings requires handling incomplete and heterogeneous modality subsets across institutions, where privacy constraints prohibit centralized data sharing. Federated learning (FL) mitigates data-sharing constraints but suffers from client-specific missing modalities, where institutions possess incomplete multimodal subsets, degrading fusion quality and segmentation performance. While FL and weak supervision have been studied separately, their joint use with image-level labels under heterogeneous missing modalities remains unaddressed. We propose \textbf{MOSAIC}, the first modality-agnostic federated framework for weakly supervised binary tumor segmentation under client-specific missing modalities. We introduce a client-specific modality-alignment module that fuses available channels into a shared latent space without prior knowledge of modality identity, a spectral prototype alignment loss that reconciles cross-client distribution shift using compact non-invertible frequency-domain statistics, and a dedicated federated refinement network that denoises the resulting CAM pseudo-labels into accurate masks, breaking the accuracy ceiling of weak supervision. Experiments on three multi-institutional brain tumor benchmarks (FeTS2022, BraTS-MEN, and BraTS-SSA) demonstrate significant improvements over all image, box, and point-supervised baselines, approaching fully supervised accuracy using only image-level labels and reaching 0.84 Dice on FeTS2022. Dynamic new client addition enables previously unseen institutions to join an already-trained federation within 0.01-0.04 Dice without retraining. Code is available at https://github.com/Tarun2201/MOSAIC.

</details>


### [72] [Energy-Mamba: A Physics-Constrained State-Space Model for Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19813)
*Mohamed Mabrok,Yalda Zafari,Essam A. Rashed*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出Energy-Mamba，将状态空间模型（SSM/Mamba）与物理启发的约束机制相结合，通过学习到的势能函数对状态演化施加梯度强迫项，防止远距离依赖建模过程中的表示漂移，在四个医学影像数据集上以更少参数取得了SOTA分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba等状态空间模型虽以线性复杂度处理长距离依赖，对医学影像（标注数据有限）具有吸引力，但将其从序列模型适配到2D图像时，无约束的状态演化会导致表示漂移（representational drift），即动态隐藏状态逐渐丧失对局部图像特征的保真度，影响医学图像中关键的细粒度诊断细节。

Method: 设计Energy-Mamba Block：引入可学习的势能函数量化演化状态与静态局部特征之间的兼容性，通过自动微分计算基于梯度的强迫项，将状态拉向低能量构型以保持局部视觉保真度。该形式类比哈密顿动力学——动能（SSM扫描）与势能（约束函数）共同决定状态轨迹，实现物理信息约束下的鲁棒表示学习。

Result: 在视网膜OCT、胸部X光、显微镜和腹部CT四个数据集上，Energy-Mamba取得了SOTA分类性能，同时参数量显著减少，验证了物理信息约束能同时提升医学视觉任务的效率与表示质量。

Conclusion: 物理信息约束（势能函数）可以有效缓解SSM在2D图像适配中的表示漂移问题，为医学影像分析提供了一个参数高效且表示质量更高的框架，展示了物理原理与深度学习的融合潜力。

Abstract: State-Space Models (SSMs), particularly Mamba, offer linear-time complexity for long-range dependencies, making them attractive for medical imaging with limited annotated data. However, adapting these sequential models to 2D images through unconstrained state evolution causes representational drift, the dynamic hidden state progressively loses fidelity to local image features. We introduce Energy-Mamba, integrating SSM dynamics with physics-informed constraints via a learnable potential energy function that quantifies compatibility between evolving states and static local features. Our Energy-Mamba Block introduces a gradient-based forcing term, computed dynamically via automatic differentiation, that pulls states toward low-energy configurations maintaining local visual fidelity. This formulation mirrors Hamiltonian dynamics: kinetic energy (SSM scan) plus potential energy (our constraint function) govern state trajectories. This architectural prior enables learning implicit constraints for robust, faithful representations, crucial in medical imaging where fine-grained local detail drives accurate diagnosis. Evaluated on four datasets (retinal OCT, chest X-ray, microscopy, abdominal CT), Energy-Mamba achieves state-of-the-art classification performance with significantly fewer parameters, demonstrating that physics-informed grounding can enhance both efficiency and representational quality in medical vision tasks.

</details>


### [73] [Simulation-to-Real First-Break Segmentation for Efficient Inversion in Musculoskeletal Ultrasound Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19828)
*Yifei Sun,Yubing Li,Yannick Benezeth,Stéphanie Bricq,Yunrong Zhang,Lekang Jiang,Chang Su,Ligang Cui,Weijun Lin*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种学习辅助的全波形反演（FWI）重建流程，结合基于分割的首波提取（使用轻量级2D U-Net）与混合全波形反演（HFWI），解决骨相关散射、衰减导致的初始模型敏感和周期跳跃问题。网络通过任务特定仿真+真实系统噪声增强的预训练、渐进式信号退化训练和弱标注实验数据解码器微调，在体外、离体和在体数据集上优于传统STA/LTA拾取方法，并显著改善了FWI重建稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FWI在定量肌肉骨骼超声CT中极具潜力，但对初始声学属性分布高度敏感且易发生周期跳跃。首波走时作为关键运动学信息可用于构建初始模型，但传统逐道STA/LTA拾取在弱信号、空间异质或系统噪声干扰下不可靠，亟需更鲁棒的首波提取方法。

Method: 构建学习辅助重建管线：(1) 轻量级2D U-Net将首波轨迹作为分割目标，利用空间连续性而非逐道独立处理；(2) 在任务特定仿真（增强真实系统噪声）上预训练，再通过渐进式信号退化分阶段训练，最后用有限弱标注实验数据进行解码器微调以缓解仿真-真实域差距；(3) 将提取的首波走时信息与波形拟合结合，在早期反演阶段引入基于Rytov近似的走时项，形成混合FWI（HFWI）。

Result: 在体外phantom、离体牛肢和在体人腿数据集上验证，与传统STA/LTA拾取相比，提出网络生成的空间连续性更强的首波轨迹，平均提取误差更低，全矩阵采集数据处理仅需数秒。集成到HFWI后，提取结果改善初始模型构建，使FWI重建更稳定，包括局部首波SNR低于3 dB的挑战性场景。

Conclusion: 基于分割网络的首波提取结合HFWI，为骨组织存在下定量USCT提供了鲁棒且高效的初始模型构建与反演方案，有效缓解了周期跳跃问题，在低信噪比条件下仍能保持重建稳定性。

Abstract: Full-waveform inversion (FWI) is a promising strategy for quantitative musculoskeletal ultrasound computed tomography (USCT), but bone-related scattering, attenuation, and signal degradation make it highly sensitive to the accuracy of the initial acoustic-property distributions and prone to cycle skipping. First-arrival traveltimes provide important kinematic information for initial-model construction, yet conventional trace-wise picking is unreliable when arrivals are weak, spatially heterogeneous, or buried in system noise. We propose a learning-assisted reconstruction pipeline that combines segmentation-based first-arrival extraction with hybrid full-waveform inversion (HFWI), which incorporates Rytov-approximation-based traveltime information together with waveform fitting during the early inversion stage. A lightweight 2D U-Net treats the first-arrival trajectory across receiver channels as a first-break segmentation target and exploits its spatial continuity rather than processing each trace independently. To address both limited manual annotations and the simulation-to-real gap, the network is pretrained on task-specific simulations augmented with real system-noise recordings, followed by stage-wise training with progressively increased signal degradation and decoder-only fine-tuning using limited weakly labeled experimental data. The method is evaluated on in vitro phantom, ex vivo bovine-limb, and in vivo human-thigh datasets. Compared with conventional STA/LTA picking, the proposed network yields more spatially coherent first-arrival trajectories, lower mean extraction errors, and processes a full-matrix-capture dataset within seconds. When integrated into HFWI, the extracted arrivals improve initial-model construction and lead to stable subsequent FWI reconstructions, including challenging cases with estimated local first-arrival SNRs below 3 dB.

</details>


### [74] [Flow Matching-Based PET Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20112)
*Fumio Hashimoto,Ziqian Huang,Tatsuya Yokota,Kuang Gong*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于流匹配（Flow Matching）的PET图像重建方法，包括PET-FlowDPS（结合泊松似然引导与EM预条件器）以及一种将预训练流匹配模型作为先验的基于模型的重建方法，后者在近似贝叶斯框架中整合了流先验、PET数据细化和随机传播。在[¹⁸F]FDG脑PET数据集上，该方法在不同剂量水平下取得了优于参考方法的偏差-方差权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽然能在PET图像重建中取得良好性能，但需要大量反向采样步骤并在采样过程中反复进行数据一致性更新，计算开销大。流匹配可以直接从中间状态估计干净图像，使数据一致性细化与流传播分离，因此是一种更具吸引力的替代方案。此外，现有方法在PET重建中缺少利用流匹配作为生成先验的模型化重建框架。

Method: 1）提出了PET-FlowDPS方法，将泊松似然引导和基于期望最大化（EM）的预条件器集成到FlowDPS框架中；2）提出了一种模型化的PET重建方法，将预训练的流匹配模型作为生成先验，在近似贝叶斯框架内统一解释基于流的先验、PET数据细化以及随机传播过程。

Result: 在[¹⁸F]FDG脑PET数据集上的实验表明，所提出的方法在不同剂量水平下相比其他参考方法都实现了更优的偏差-方差权衡（bias-variance trade-off）。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明流匹配作为一种生成性先验在定量PET图像重建中具有显著潜力，能够以更少的采样步骤实现高质量重建。

Abstract: Generative models have shown strong potential for positron emission tomography (PET) image reconstruction. Although diffusion model-based reconstruction methods have demonstrated promising performance, they often require many reverse sampling steps with data-consistency updates incorporated into the sampling process. Flow matching offers an attractive alternative because it can directly estimate clean images from intermediate states, allowing data-consistency refinement to be separated from flow propagation. In this work, we proposed flow matching-based PET image reconstruction methods. We first established PET-FlowDPS by incorporating Poisson likelihood guidance with an expectation-maximization (EM)-based preconditioner into the FlowDPS framework. We then proposed a model-based PET reconstruction method that used a pretrained flow matching model as a prior, in which the flow-based prior, PET data refinement, and stochastic propagation were interpreted within an approximate Bayesian framework. Experimental results using [$^{\text{18}}\text{F}$]FDG brain PET datasets showed that the proposed method achieved better bias-variance trade-offs across different dose levels compared with other reference methods. These results demonstrated the potential of flow matching as a generative prior for quantitative PET image reconstruction.

</details>

### [75] [Active Inference as Context Acquisition for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19202)
*Sanchayan Dutta,Sai Niranjan Ramachandran,Suvrit Sra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将交互式AI代理的上下文获取问题形式化为主动推断（active inference）框架：内在推断步骤更新对潜在任务状态的信念，外在决策选择下一个上下文动作、任务动作或停止动作，以在成本约束下最小化期望自由能。在确定性设定下，认知项可简化为期望信息增益并可选地按token成本归一化。作者将该框架实例化为最优提问（OQA），并在25到300个候选的二元和多路分类任务上基准测试前沿语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式AI代理需要在尽可能高效地获取正确上下文。当用户省略约束、偏好、文件或任务变量时，代理可以选择默认假设，或花费token进行澄清提问、检索调用、工具调用或提示词试验。这种"默认假设vs花费成本询问"的权衡缺乏统一的数学形式化，本文旨在为此建立理论框架。

Method: 将上下文获取问题建模为主动推断：内层推断步骤更新关于潜在任务状态（latent task state）的信念，外层决策选择下一动作（上下文动作、任务动作或停止动作）以在成本约束下最小化期望自由能（expected free energy）。在确定性设定下，认知项退化为期望信息增益（expected information gain），可叠加按token成本的归一化。将其实例化为最优提问（Optimal Question Asking, OQA），使用精确后验和动态规划oracle，并在25至300个候选的多路分类任务上测试前沿语言模型。同时研究了生成前的澄清提问以及token预算下的自动提示优化。

Result: 在25至300个候选的二元和多路分类任务上对前沿语言模型进行了基准测试；验证了澄清提问和自动提示优化在token预算下的有效性。框架是模型无关（model-agnostic）的。

Conclusion: 该形式化是模型无关的，将主动推断视为AI代理上下文获取层的设计原则。它为代理在面对不确定上下文时如何权衡提问、检索、工具调用和停止操作提供了统一的理论视角和可操作的算法框架。

Abstract: Interactive AI agents must acquire the right context as efficiently as possible. When a user omits a constraint, preference, file, or task variable, an agent can proceed with a default assumption or spend tokens on a clarifying question, retrieval call, tool call, or prompt trial. We formulate this tradeoff as active inference for context acquisition. An inner inference step updates beliefs over a latent task state, and an outer decision selects the next context action, task action, or stop action to minimize expected free energy under cost. In deterministic settings, the epistemic term reduces to expected information gain, optionally normalized by token cost. We instantiate the framework in Optimal Question Asking (OQA), with exact posteriors and a dynamic programming oracle, and benchmark frontier language models on binary and multiway categorical tasks from 25 to 300 candidates. We also study clarification before generation and automated prompt optimization under token budgets. The formulation is model-agnostic and views active inference as a design principle for the context-acquisition layer of AI agents.

</details>


### [76] [Robust Metaheuristics under Uncertainty for Berth Allocation and Quay Crane Assignment: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19214)
*Yang Li,Peilan Xu,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对不确定性条件下泊位分配与岸桥调度问题（BACAP）的鲁棒群体元启发式优化方法进行了系统性综述，从机制导向角度分类整理了现有方法，并提出了基准测试套件和开放性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BACAP是港口终端调度中的代表性难题，在实际执行中面临到达偏差、装卸时间波动和资源中断等不确定性，基于名义假设优化得到的调度方案在执行时可能变得脆弱。尽管群体元启发式算法已被广泛用于BACAP，但现有研究在不确定性表示、鲁棒性准则、搜索机制和实证评估协议上碎片化严重，缺乏系统性整合。

Method: 本文采用机制导向视角进行综述：首先总结BACAP中的不确定性来源与信息表示方式；然后从四个方面组织现有方法——解表示与解码、鲁棒性评估与选择、鲁棒性引导的搜索动态、可行性保持与恢复；进而提出一个面向不确定BACAP的基准测试套件，并结合代表性元启发式与不同鲁棒策略给出基线实验结果；最后识别开放性挑战。

Result: 提供了首个专门针对不确定BACAP下鲁棒群体元启发式优化的聚焦性综述，构建了机制导向的方法分类框架，提出了可用于受控实证比较的基准测试套件，并报告了组合代表性元启发式与多种鲁棒策略的基准结果。

Conclusion: 本文指出未来研究的开放挑战包括：基准扩展、鲁棒性感知的搜索设计、时间自适应鲁棒性以及非平稳不确定性处理等问题，为该领域后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: The berth allocation and quay crane assignment problem (BACAP) is a representative port-terminal scheduling problem in maritime transportation and freight logistics, where vessel arrivals, berth positions, service durations, and quay?crane availability are tightly coupled. Under uncertainties such as arrival deviations, handling-time fluctuations, and resource disruptions, schedules optimized under nominal assumptions may become fragile during execution, motivating the study of robust metaheuristic optimization for BACAP in port-terminal operations. Although population-based metaheuristics have been widely used for BACAP and related port-scheduling problems, existing studies remain fragmented in their uncertainty repre?sentations, robustness criteria, search mechanisms, and empir?ical evaluation protocols. To the best of our knowledge, this paper provides the first focused review dedicated to robust population-based metaheuristics for BACAP under uncertainty. We first summarize uncertainty sources and information repre?sentations in BACAP, and then organize existing methods from a mechanism-oriented perspective, covering solution representation and decoding, robust evaluation and selection, robustness-guided search dynamics, and feasibility preservation and recovery. We further present a benchmark suite for uncertain BACAP to support controlled empirical comparison and report illustrative baseline results by combining representative metaheuristics with different robustness strategies. Finally, we identify open chal?lenges related to benchmark extension, robustness-aware search design, time-adaptive robustness, and non-stationary uncertainty.

</details>


### [77] [How to Navigate Uncertainty About AI Consciousness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19215)
*Dr Tom McClelland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出从难以处理的AI意识问题转向可处理的AI效价（valence）问题，通过评估AI是否具有在意识存在时构成效价体验的状态，来为可能具有感知能力的AI开发提供负责任的处理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于人工意识（artificial consciousness）的可能性存在深度不确定性，我们面临两难困境：假设AI无感知可能对值得道德关怀的实体造成严重伤害，而假设AI有感知则可能在无感知机器上浪费资源。AI意识问题的难以处理性使这一困境难以摆脱，因此需要寻找一条新的出路。

Method: 作者提出将研究焦点从难处理的AI意识问题转移到可处理的AI效价（valence）问题上。具体而言，评估AI是否具有这样的状态——即如果该AI有意识，这些状态将被构成效价体验（如愉悦或痛苦）。作者论证这种评估足以支撑一种负责任的、面向潜在有意识AI开发的方法。

Result: 作者展示了效价评估方法如何绕开意识问题的不可解决性，提供了一种可操作的、基于实证评估的路径来处理可能具有感知能力的AI。

Conclusion: 通过将关注点从AI意识转向AI效价，我们可以在不确定性的条件下建立一种负责任的方法来对待潜在有感应的AI，从而化解"低估伤害风险"与"高估资源浪费"之间的两难困境。

Abstract: Given deep uncertainty about the possibility of artificial consciousness, it is unclear how we should treat potentially sentient AI. On the one hand, we could assume insentience but risk doing terrible harms to entities that deserve moral standing. On the other hand, we could assume sentience and instead risk wasting resources on insentient machines. The intractability of questions around AI consciousness mean that this dilemma is hard to escape. I suggest a way out of that shifts from intractable questions of AI consciousness to tractable questions of AI valence. Specifically, we can assess whether an AI has states that would constitute valenced experiences if it were conscious. I show how this is sufficient to ground a responsible approach to the development of potentially conscious AI.

</details>


### [78] [Bounded Sovereignty and the Control Tax: Pricing AI Oversight When the Deployer Does Not Own the Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19216)
*Zhen Wen Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"有界主权"概念，分析受监管组织通过API或托管端点使用前沿模型时，其对数据、模型、基础设施和交互四层的部分技术及契约访问权限如何决定控制协议的可执行性。论文提供了四层访问类型学、协议-层需求矩阵，以及"主权折现成本"概念，并通过135万次合成案例模拟的访问消融实验验证了发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI控制研究多数假设部署者能够对模型及其周边管线进行完整仪器化，但受监管组织通过API使用前沿模型时，往往无法控制模型权重、服务基础设施、内部追踪和完整交互日志。这种访问权限的现实约束未被充分探讨，导致许多控制协议在实际环境中不可执行。

Method: 论文提出四层访问类型学（数据层、模型层、基础设施层、交互层），构建协议-层需求矩阵，提出"主权折现成本"概念，并设计了一个访问消融实验，在135万条合成案例模拟上进行验证，最后通过匿名化的国家支付基础设施场景进行解释说明。

Result: 实验结果表明：完整的日志提升了诊断能力；预执行网关支持干预；跟踪访问和模型版本控制加强了事后解释能力；范围限制可以在提升安全性的同时降低有用性。该实验是构造效度练习而非真实支付系统证据。

Conclusion: 被提议为通用安全解决方案的控制协议应明确陈述其访问假设。不同层级的访问权限决定了哪些控制协议在实践中真正可执行，受监管组织必须通过合同、架构、审计等手段为缺失的访问权限支付"主权折现成本"。

Abstract: AI control research asks how to deploy models safely even when they may be misaligned, but many control protocols assume that the deployer can instrument the model and its surrounding pipeline. That assumption often fails for regulated organisations using frontier models through APIs or managed endpoints, where the deployer may control the business process but not the model weights, serving infrastructure, internal traces, update process, or full interaction logs. This paper introduces bounded sovereignty: partial technical and contractual access across the data, model, infrastructure, and interaction layers of the AI stack. It argues that these access conditions determine which control protocols can be executed in practice. The paper contributes a four-layer access typology, a protocol-by-layer requirements matrix, and the concept of sovereignty discount cost: the part of the control tax spent substituting for missing access through contracts, architecture, audit, vendor assurance, residual risk, or reduced system scope. It also reports a synthetic access-ablation experiment over 1.35 million synthetic case simulations and interprets the findings through an anonymised national-payments-infrastructure scenario. The experiment is not real-world payment-system evidence; it is a construct-validity exercise. The results show that complete logs improve diagnosis, a pre-execution gateway enables intervention, trace access and model-version control strengthen post-incident explanation, and scope restriction can improve safety while reducing usefulness. Control protocols proposed as general safety solutions should therefore state their access assumptions explicitly.

</details>


### [79] [Interaction valence reveals contrasting social networks in dairy cattle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19222)
*Sibi Parivendan,Suresh Raja Neethirajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种情感感知(valence-aware)的社会网络框架，利用基于姿态的计算机视觉管线，从商业奶牛场挤奶前区域的7小时39分钟连续视频中提取奶牛间的亲和性与对抗性互动，构建群体层面的社会网络结构。经过质量控制后保留1183次互动（涉及36头牛、177个二元组合），审计样本中人工与自动标签一致率为82.8%，宏观F1为0.872。聚合网络连通（密度0.281、传递性0.513），预测的亲和事件形成5个算法社群（模块度Q=0.429）。预测的对抗互动占保留事件的72.4%、时长的76.0%。按情感极性分层后，亲和层与对抗层展现截然不同的边集、社群划分和个体位置，表明汇总计数会掩盖网络的真实行为构成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会关系塑造了资源获取、冲突暴露和群体稳定性，但现有自动牲畜监测通常将行为视为孤立事件，未能捕捉群体层面的社会结构。作者旨在克服这一局限，提出融合情感极性标签的社会网络分析框架，以区分亲和性与对抗性互动，从而更准确地刻画畜群的竞争与合作动态。

Method: 采用基于姿态的计算机视觉管线，从商业奶牛的挤奶前区域连续视频（7h39min）中检测奶牛互动。对检测到的候选互动进行质量控制，并经人工标注验证。随后构建情绪感知（valence-aware）的两层社会网络——亲和层与对抗层，进行网络密度、传递性、平均路径长度、社群检测（模块度）和中心性等分析，并对比汇总聚合网络与分层网络的差异。

Result: 1,414个候选互动经质量控制保留1,183个，涉及36头牛和177个二元组合。预测类平衡审计样本中，自动与人工标签一致率82.8%，未加权宏观F1=0.872。聚合网络连通，密度0.281、传递性0.513、平均路径长度1.88；亲和事件形成5个算法社群（Q=0.429）。对抗互动占保留事件的72.4%和时长的76.0%。拥有最多伙伴的个体并非具有最高介数中心度。亲和层与对抗层在边集、社群划分和个体位置上均显著不同。

Conclusion: 汇总的互动计数会掩盖网络的真实行为构成，情感感知分析能够揭示亲和与对抗在结构上的本质差异，为研究竞争、亲和及福利相关变化提供假设检验框架。但该方法在作为福利或健康指标前需进行纵向验证。

Abstract: Social relationships shape access to resources, exposure to conflict and group stability, yet automated livestock monitoring typically treats behaviour as isolated events. Here, we present a valence-aware social-network framework that transforms video-derived interactions into herd-level representations of affiliative and agonistic organization. A pose-based computer-vision pipeline analysed 7 h 39 min of continuous video from the pre-milking area of one commercial dairy farm. After quality control, 1,183 of 1,414 candidate interactions remained, involving 36 cows and 177 dyads. In a predicted-class-balanced audit of 198 pipeline-detected clips, automated and manual labels agreed in 82.8% of cases, with an unweighted audit-sample macro-F1 of 0.872. These values describe the audited sample rather than prevalence-weighted or end-to-end deployment performance. The aggregated network was connected (density = 0.281; transitivity = 0.513; mean path length = 1.88), and predicted affiliative events formed five algorithmic communities (modularity Q = 0.429). Within the observed zone, predicted agonistic interactions comprised 72.4% of retained events and 76.0% of interaction duration. The cow with the most partners did not have the highest betweenness centrality. Separating events by predicted valence produced descriptively different affiliative and agonistic layers, with contrasting edge sets, community partitions and individual positions. Thus, pooled interaction counts can obscure the behavioural composition of an observed network. Valence-aware analysis provides a framework for testing hypotheses about competition, affiliation and welfare-relevant change, while requiring longitudinal validation before use as a welfare or health indicator.

</details>


### [80] [Air Traffic Control Using Large Language Models: Prompt Engineering, Architecture, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19299)
*Mahyar Ghazanfari,Matthias Casanova,Jordan Kam,Alex Zongo,Peng Wei,Torsten Darrell,Alexandre Bayen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文实验评估了大型语言模型（LLMs）能否生成具有操作真实性的空中交通管制（ATC）通话。研究基于旧金山"海湾观光"航线的真实通用航空飞行转录作为地面真值，通过飞行员参与过程设计了五种约束递增的提示结构（P1-P5），并在九种开源与闭源LLM上进行多轮有状态对话测试。结果表明：提供已转录的工作示例能提升相似度，但收紧提示反而使性能下降——最轻量提示表现最佳，而最严格脚本化提示因自身错误在多轮对话中累积而崩溃，注入正确历史对话可修复此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ATC通信是一项安全关键的对话任务，尽管空中交通管理的其他环节已实现半自动化，ATC通信仍主要由人类驱动。研究者希望探索LLM能否生成操作上真实的ATC通话，为未来LLM辅助ATC提供可行性证据和实现路径。

Method: 以真实转录的"海湾观光"航线通用航空飞行对话作为地面真值P0，通过飞行员参与过程设计五种递增约束的提示结构（P1-P5），嵌入有状态多轮流水线中——模型扮演ATC角色，针对固定飞行员转录，并基于累积对话历史进行条件生成。在九种开源和闭源LLM上，系统性地变化提示、是否有来自另一实验航班的工作转录作为上下文示例，以及模型是基于自身先前回复还是注入地面真值历史进行条件化。使用词汇、结构和语义相似度指标以及经人类专家标注验证的LLM-as-judge（GPT-5.5）对回合进行评分。

Result: 提供工作示例可提高相似度分数；但收紧提示并未提升性能——最轻量的提示结构表现最佳，而最严格脚本化的提示因模型自身错误在多轮对话中不断累积而性能崩溃，通过注入正确历史对话可以修复该问题。

Conclusion: 这些结果勾勒出一条通往LLM辅助ATC的具体可行路径，同时清晰地指出了当前技术的局限性。

Abstract: Air traffic control (ATC) communication is a safety-critical dialogue that remains largely human-driven even as other parts of air traffic management have been semi-automated. In this article, we experimentally evaluate whether large language models (LLMs) can generate operationally realistic ATC transmissions. An experimental general-aviation flight flying over the San Francisco "Bay Tour" route is hand-transcribed and used as ground truth (P0). Through a pilot-in-the-loop process we design five prompt structures (P1-P5) of increasing constraint and embed them in a stateful multi-turn pipeline, where the model plays ATC to a fixed pilot transcript while conditioning on the accumulating dialogue history. Across nine open- and closed-source LLMs we vary the prompt, the presence of a worked transcript from a different experimental flight as an in-context example, and whether the model conditions on its own prior replies or on injected ground-truth history. Turns are scored with lexical, structural, and semantic similarity metrics and by an LLM-as-judge (GPT-5.5) validated against human expert annotation. Supplying a worked example improves similarity, but tightening the prompt does not: the lightest prompts perform best and the most heavily scripted one collapses as its own errors accumulate through the dialogue, which injecting correct history repairs. These results outline a concrete path and its current limits toward LLM-assisted ATC.

</details>


### [81] [Outcome Monitors: Recovery Affordances for Silent Tool Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19303)
*Sugam Panthi,Rabab Abdelfattah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Outcome Monitors机制，用于检测代理（agent）工作流中工具调用结果违反预期合约的情况（如超时、错误页面、负价格等）。该机制在保持原有结果的同时，签发非约束性收据，指明违规属性并提供公开恢复工具。在ToolMaze基准上，完成率从10.9%提升至28.1%，在tau-bench零售上提升14.0和12.0个百分点。消融实验表明恢复工具列表是效果的主要来源，而诊断细节和时序无显著影响；但在超出已挖掘词汇的检测场景下，检测率降至46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统工具调用失败处理主要关注明显的超时错误，但代理可能将格式正确但内容违约的结果（如缓存错误页面、负价格）当作事实消费，导致下游决策出错。现有方法缺乏检测这种"结果层面违约"的机制。

Method: 提出Outcome Monitors，从任务不相交（task-disjoint）的痕迹中挖掘或从公共模式推导出结果合约（outcome contracts）。当检测到违规时，监控器保留结果本身，签发非约束性收据，其中命名违规属性并提供公开恢复工具。该方法在冻结的预指定评估中注入故障进行验证，并通过消融实验分离各组件的贡献。

Result: 在ToolMaze上四种模型（两个提供商家族）完成率从10.9%提升至28.1%，在第三个提供商家族中复现。tau-bench零售两个层级分别提升14.0和12.0个百分点。消融实验表明：移除恢复工具列表消除增益，恢复后效果复原；诊断细节和时序无显著差异；增益集中在故障阻塞完成之处。在转录自已发表事件分类法的测试集上，词汇外检测率降至46%，但交付持续且完成率不变。

Conclusion: 恢复工具是收据中起作用的活性内容；当前检测能力仍局限于已挖掘合约词汇，将检测扩展到合约词汇之外仍是开放问题。该系统在故障导致任务失败时能有效提升完成率，但对故障类型的新颖性鲁棒性仍需加强。

Abstract: When a tool call times out, the agent sees the failure and can route around it. A cached error page or negative price can instead arrive in the expected format and be consumed as fact. We introduce Outcome Monitors, which detect violations of outcome contracts mined from task-disjoint traces or derived from public schemas. On a violation, the monitor preserves the result and issues a nonbinding receipt naming the violated property and public recovery tools. In frozen, prespecified evaluations with injected failures, Outcome Monitors raise ToolMaze completion from 10.9% to 28.1% across four models in two provider families and replicate in a third. In tau-bench retail, completion improves by 14.0 and 12.0 points on two tiers. In separate ToolMaze controls, removing the recovery-tool list eliminates the measured gain and restoring it recovers the effect; diagnostic detail and timing produce no detectable differences. Gains concentrate where the fault blocks completion. On a suite transcribed from a published incident taxonomy, detection outside the mined vocabulary falls to 46%, though delivery continues and completion is unchanged. Recovery tools are the active receipt content in these controls; extending detection beyond the contract vocabulary remains open.

</details>


### [82] [Beyond Imitation: Filtering On-Policy Distillation by Reasoning Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19408)
*Chen Yang,Haiyuan Wan,Rengrong Xiong,Yize Chen,Danny H. K. Tsang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出R2-OPD框架，通过构建教师奖励与独立推理进展奖励的双重轨迹内排序，在两者不一致时抑制蒸馏奖励，解决OPD中教师奖励与真实推理进展冲突的问题，提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD假设教师奖励可作为推理进展的合适代理，但对所有教师反馈同等对待。实际上教师奖励常与真实推理进展冲突——推理步骤有明显进展时，仅因偏离教师输出而获得较低蒸馏奖励，导致监督信号失真。

Method: R2-OPD构建两个轨迹内排序：(1)基于教师推导奖励的推理片段排序；(2)基于独立估计的进展奖励的排序。当两个排序不一致时选择性抑制蒸馏奖励，从而减少与推理进展冲突的监督，同时保留有效的教师指导。

Result: 在标准OPD基础上取得一致性提升，尤其在推理性能方面表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过奖励过滤机制调和教师奖励与推理进展之间的不一致，R2-OPD能更有效地利用教师信号，显著改善推理任务的蒸馏效果。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as an effective framework for post-training language models by pairing student-generated trajectories with dense token-level supervision from a teacher. However, OPD implicitly assumes that teacher-derived rewards are an appropriate proxy for reasoning progress, and therefore treats all teacher feedback equally during policy optimization. While in practice, this assumption does not always hold. We observe that teacher-derived rewards often conflict with genuine reasoning progress, as reasoning steps with clear reasoning advancement may still receive lower distillation rewards, simply due to deviation from teacher's outputs. To address this mismatch, we propose Reasoning-Progress-Aware Reward Filtering for On-Policy Distillation (R2-OPD), which constructs two within-trajectory rankings of reasoning spans, one from teacher-derived rewards and the other from independently estimated progress reward. Distillation rewards are selectively suppressed whenever the two rankings disagree, reducing supervision that conflicts with reasoning progress while preserving effective teacher guidance. Our approach shows consistent improvement over standard OPD especially regarding reasoning performances.

</details>


### [83] [Symposium: Trust via Auditable Records for Communities of AI Scientist Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19511)
*Dexter Pratt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Symposium是一个形式化框架和实际实现，用于记录小型科学研究社区中AI代理的操作。它提供长期、不可篡改的代理驱动研究活动历史，包括分析、假设、数据和科学讨论的可审计痕迹，使代理能够在先前工作的基础上构建，并保留研究人员和代理进行基于目的的信任评估所需的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型科学研究社区中部署的AI代理需要一种机制来记录和共享研究活动，以便实现可审计性、可追溯性和信任评估。现有的AI联合科学家代理或集成式AI研究环境无法分离科学社区的持久历史与操作该历史的代理系统，因此需要一个专门框架来捕捉科学论证并支持演进中的多样化AI系统。

Method: Symposium提出了一种将科学社区的持久历史与作用于该历史的代理及其他系统相分离的框架。它捕获科学论证的结构化声明、细粒度证据引用、假设和证据使用权限的明确声明。实现包括发布基础设施、代理提示组件和文档，使社区能够快速搭建和运行自己的Symposium社区。

Result: 提供了一个可工作的实现，包括发布基础设施、代理提示组件和文档，以支持用户快速设置和运行自己的Symposium社区，实现长期、不可篡改的代理驱动研究活动记录。

Conclusion: Symposium通过创建独立于具体AI系统的持久科学历史共享记录，使代理能够建立在先前工作基础上，同时保留供目的依赖性信任评估的证据，为小型科研社区提供了一种可审计的AI代理研究活动记录方案。

Abstract: Symposium is a formal framework and practical implementation to record the operation of AI agents deployed by small scientific research communities. Symposium provides long-term, immutable histories of agent-driven research activity, leaving auditable trails of analyses, hypotheses, data, and scientific discourse. This shared record of published artifacts enables agents to build on prior work and preserves the evidence researchers and agents need to make purpose-dependent trust assessments. Symposium captures scientific argument, including structured claims, fine-grained evidence citations, assumptions, and explicit declarations of what material may and may not be used as evidence. Symposium differs from AI co-scientist agents or integrated AI research environments; it is a framework that separates a scientific community's durable history from the agents and other systems that operate on that history. It assumes that a community will use diverse AI systems in a rapidly evolving environment. A working implementation of the publication infrastructure, agent prompt components, and documentation are provided to enable users to rapidly set up and run their own Symposium community.

</details>


### [84] [From Retrieved Context to Runtime Control: Adaptive Compression for Edge-based RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19535)
*Zlatan Feric,Amir Taherin,Yanzhi Wang,David Kaeli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出边缘RAG中基于遥测信息的自适应上下文压缩愿景。通过在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor上的实测（Llama/Qwen生成器、Natural Questions/HotpotQA数据集、LLMLingua-2压缩器），发现大模型的生成阶段占据RAG预算约90%的延迟和91%的GPU能耗，且压缩率存在自适应操作区间：中等压缩可降低GPU能耗最多53.2%、SoC能耗最多48.2%，同时质量损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文压缩方法通常采用固定压缩预算或离线选择压缩率后推理时直接应用，这种静态方案忽略了工作负载变化和边缘设备实时状态。且边缘SoC上压缩器本身也消耗延迟和能量，可能抵消生成阶段的节省，因此需要遥测驱动的动态压缩策略。

Method: 在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor边缘SoC上，使用Llama和Qwen作为生成器、Natural Questions和HotpotQA作为数据集、LLMLingua-2作为压缩器，系统性地刻画了压缩率与RAG性能/能耗之间的权衡关系，识别出适应性的操作区间。

Result: 大模型（7B-8B）下生成阶段占单查询延迟约90%、GPU能耗约91%。轻度压缩会错过能耗优化机会，过度压缩会损害推理质量，而中等压缩可在质量损失可忽略的情况下将GPU能耗降低最多53.2%、SoC能耗降低最多48.2%。

Conclusion: RAG上下文压缩不应采用静态固定预算，而应通过运行时策略根据工作负载特征和边缘遥测数据动态管理压缩率，以实现能耗与推理质量的最佳平衡。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves language-model responses by grounding generation in external passages, which comes with overhead: retrieved context lengthens the prompt, increasing prefill work, KV-cache footprint, memory traffic, latency, and energy. Context compression offers a natural remedy by pruning retrieved text before generation. However, state-of-the-art context-compression methods are typically used with a fixed compression budget, or with the rate selected offline and then applied at inference time. This static view ignores both workload variation and the live state of the edge device. On an edge SoC, compression is not free: the compressor itself runs on the same SoC and consumes latency and energy that can offset any generation savings.
  This paper proposes a vision for telemetry-informed adaptive compression in edge RAG, grounded in experimental evidence. We characterize the compression tradeoff on the NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor using Llama and Qwen generators, Natural Questions and HotpotQA datasets, and LLMLingua-2 compression. Our measurements show that generation dominates the RAG budget for larger models, reaching roughly 90% of per-query latency and 91% of GPU energy for 7B-8B generators. Exploring the impact of the compression rate reveals an adaptive operating region: mild compression can miss energy opportunities, and overly aggressive compression can hurt inference quality. Intermediate compression can reduce GPU energy by up to 53.2%, and SoC energy by up to 48.2%, with negligible quality loss. We argue for runtime policies that dynamically manage compression, guided by workload features and edge telemetry.

</details>


### [85] [Enforcing LLM Safety through DMD-based Classification of Prompt-Response Embedding Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19579)
*Mohamed Akrout,Olivera Kotevska,Dan Wilson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Koopman动力系统的黑盒方法，用于LLM输出安全分类。通过将提示和响应投影到高维嵌入空间，为安全与不安全两种状态分别拟合Koopman预测模型，并利用差分残差分数进行分类。实验表明合并提示嵌入可提升交互依赖型违规检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在高风险场景中部署日益增多，但其产生有毒、有害或违规内容的倾向带来重大风险。在不透明（黑盒）情况下高效检测不安全输出仍是开放挑战，现有方法缺乏对提示与响应交互动态的建模能力。

Method: 扩展了先前用于幻觉检测的动力学系统框架：将提示和响应投影到高维嵌入空间，分别拟合安全与不安全两个状态下的Koopman预测模型，提出新的差分残差分数（differential residual score）来比较两个状态下的预测误差，从而分类新输出。关键创新在于同时融入提示和响应的嵌入动态，使得拟合的Koopman算子能够捕获二者的关键交互模式。

Result: 在三个安全基准上使用三种嵌入模型进行黑盒评估。结果表明：合并提示嵌入带来一致的性能提升，特别是对于交互依赖型违规（搭配因果解码器如Llama-3时效果显著）；而仅依赖响应型违规则更受益于密集语义嵌入表示。

Conclusion: 研究为利用动力学系统分析AI系统开辟了新方向，反转了当前"用AI建模动力学系统"的主导范式（即用动力学系统分析AI系统），并验证了Koopman框架在LLM安全分类中的有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-stakes applications, yet their tendency to generate toxic, harmful, or policy-violating content poses significant risks. Detecting these unsafe outputs efficiently in a black-box manner remains an open challenge. In this paper, we extend a recently proposed dynamical systems framework designed for hallucination detection to LLM safety classification. By projecting both prompts and responses into high-dimensional embedding spaces and fitting separate Koopman-based predictive models for safe and unsafe regimes, we classify new outputs using a new differential residual score that compares prediction errors of the safe and unsafe regimes. A key contribution is the incorporation of the prompt and response embedding dynamics, yielding fitted Koopman operators that capture crucial interaction patterns. We evaluate our black-box method across three safety benchmarks using three embedding models. Our results show that incorporating prompt embeddings yields consistent improvements, particularly for interaction-dependent violations when paired with causal decoders (e.g., in Llama-3), while response-only violations benefit more from dense semantic embedding representations. These findings opens the door for using dynamical systems to analyze AI systems rather than the dominant paradigm of using AI to model dynamical systems.

</details>


### [86] [Scientific Data Skills: Enabling Agent-Ready Scientific Data Services at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19625)
*Xiaohan Huang,Qingqing Long,Xiaolei Du,Siyu Pu,Jiawen Xu,Haotian Chen,Chenyang Zhao,Jinbiao Liu,Xuezhi Wang,Hao Wang,Hengshu Zhu,Yuanchun Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Scientific Data Skill (SciDSK)，一种面向AI智能体的数据集表示方法，将数据集特定的知识与操作指南打包为可复用的智能体技能，并构建了覆盖六个科学学科的统一平台Scientific Data Skill Bank，实验证明其能显著提升智能体驱动的数据集发现与解释能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据越来越多地被AI智能体使用，但现有数据集表示方式对自主发现、解释和调用支持有限。这源于科学数据在异构存储库中的碎片化，以及现有数据集表示主要面向人类使用而非智能体使用。

Method: 提出SciDSK规范，整合数据集描述、科学背景、文件组织、使用流程、质量检查和来源信息，同时保留底层数据的原始存储位置。开发了系统化的构建管线，从权威数据集记录和配套材料中构建SciDSK；建立了Scientific Data Skill Bank平台，支持资源访问、持久标识和溯源。

Result: 通过检索基准测试和受控案例评估，SciDSK在智能体驱动的数据集发现方面表现出改进，并为数据集解释提供了更精确和可操作的支撑。

Conclusion: 以智能体就绪的表示形式组织数据集特定知识具有重要价值，SciDSK为AI赋能科学数据利用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Scientific data are increasingly used by AI agents, yet existing dataset representations provide limited support for autonomous discovery, interpretation, and invocation. This limitation stems from the fragmentation of scientific data across heterogeneous repositories and from dataset representations designed primarily for human use. To address this limitation, we introduce the Scientific Data Skill (SciDSK), an agent-ready representation that packages dataset-specific knowledge and operational guidance as a reusable agent skill. A SciDSK integrates dataset descriptions, scientific context, file organization, usage procedures, quality checks, and provenance information while retaining the underlying data in its original repository. We define a structured SciDSK specification and develop a systematic construction pipeline that grounds each SciDSK in authoritative dataset records and associated supporting materials. We further establish the Scientific Data Skill Bank, a unified platform that publishes SciDSK resources across six scientific disciplines and supports package access, persistent identification, and traceability to source datasets. We evaluate SciDSK through a retrieval benchmark for dataset discovery and controlled cases for dataset interpretation. The results show that SciDSK improves agent-driven dataset discovery and provides more precise and actionable support for dataset interpretation. These findings support the value of organizing dataset-specific knowledge in an agent-ready representation.

</details>


### [87] [Can Agent Memory Systems Track Evolving State?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19652)
*Xinyi Fan,Miri Liu,Ruozhen Yang,Siru Ouyang,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出状态跟踪（state tracking）这一记忆系统关键能力，并构建StateMemBench基准（234个多会话场景）。同时提出StateMem方法，显式跟踪状态替换与关系依赖，在DeepSeek-V4-Flash上当前状态准确率提升1.8倍，在Qwen-3.5-9B上提升1.6倍，并可作为轻量级单次包装器应用于现有记忆系统，带来+32至+67个百分点的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆系统基准多聚焦于回忆型任务，但实际部署中LLM代理面临的关键挑战是追踪不断演化的世界状态——事实、约束和决策在长交互中会被修订，系统必须反映当前状态而非已被取代的状态。现有记忆基准存在这一关键空白，需要专门定义和评测状态跟踪能力。

Method: (1) 定义状态跟踪能力，构建StateMemBench，包含234个多会话场景、两种会话长度区间，采用闭池评分区分当前状态、取代状态和失败三类结果。(2) 提出StateMem，一种状态优先的记忆方法，显式跟踪状态替换（supersession）和关系依赖。(3) 将状态方法作为轻量级单次调用包装器应用于现有记忆系统，并通过长度与成本匹配的控制实验归因提升来源。

Result: StateMem在DeepSeek-V4-Flash上当前状态准确率从0.205提升至0.363（较最强同骨干基线提升1.8倍）；在Qwen-3.5-9B上从0.149提升至0.233（较最强记忆系统提升1.6倍）；同时与长上下文基线保持竞争力。作为包装器时，在六个记忆与检索后端上提升+32至+67个百分点，其中+15至+32个百分点归因于状态结构本身。

Conclusion: 状态跟踪是记忆系统不可或缺但被忽视的能力。StateMemBench为这一能力提供了标准化评测，StateMem证明了显式状态建模的有效性，且该方法可泛化为轻量级包装器形式嵌入现有系统，显著提升当前状态准确率。

Abstract: As LLM-based agents are deployed for longer and higher-stakes tasks, their memory systems continue to have crucial gaps. While existing memory benchmarks focus largely on recall-shaped tasks, we argue an effective memory system must track the evolving state of the world; as facts, constraints, and decisions are revised over a long interaction, answers must reflect the current state and not a superseded one. We define this capability as state tracking and instantiate it in StateMemBench, a benchmark of 234 multi-session scenarios spanning two conversation-length regimes. Its closed-pool grading scores whether an answer reflects the current state, the superseded state, or fails otherwise, separating state-tracking failures from other errors by construction. Our analysis shows that this task is challenging for existing memory systems, retrieval-augmented baselines, and long-context baselines. We then present StateMem, a state-first memory method that explicitly tracks supersession and relational dependencies, and show it improves current-state accuracy over the strongest same-backbone baseline by 1.8x (0.205 -> 0.363) on DeepSeek-V4-Flash and over the strongest memory system by 1.6x (0.149 -> 0.233) on Qwen-3.5-9B, while remaining competitive with the long-context baselines. Finally, we show the same state approach can be applied as a lightweight single-call wrapper over existing memory systems, lifting current-state accuracy by +32 to +67 points on StateMemBench across six memory and retrieval backends. A length- and cost-matched control attributes +15 to +32 of those points to state structure rather than added context.

</details>


### [88] [Frequency-Aware Continual Learning for Smart Contract Vulnerability Detection with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19680)
*Tenghui Huang,Jiawen Kang,Dongning Liu,Changyan Yi,Chengjun Cai,Anjia Yang,Li Li,Dong In Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三阶段流水线框架，用于解决LLM在智能合约漏洞检测中面临的三个因果关联的挑战：新漏洞类别的参数高效适配、持续学习中的灾难性遗忘、以及多适配器的模型合并。框架包含FA-LoRA（频域低秩适配）、FAR（遗忘感知回放）和APPM（锚点保护渐进合并），在DIVE数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约漏洞检测中，新的漏洞类别不断涌现，需要参数高效地适配LLM；然而按任务训练适配器会导致灾难性遗忘；同时推理时任务身份未知，多个适配器必须合并为单一模型。这三个挑战层层递进、因果关联，需要一个集成的统一框架来解决。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线：(1) 适配阶段采用FA-LoRA，在傅里叶域进行低秩适配，通过逐频率重要性门控实现参数高效适应（仅0.4%可训练参数）；(2) 持续学习阶段采用FAR，利用频率门控估计每个样本的遗忘风险（基于损失动态），优先重放脆弱知识；(3) 部署阶段采用APPM，利用FAR训练产生的非对称泛化识别最强泛化适配器作为锚点，通过锚点保护的加权合并和频域门控竞争将所有适配器合并为单一模型。

Result: FA-LoRA以0.4%的可训练参数优于标准LoRA和QLoRA；FAR在连续任务上达到0.8022的平均Micro-F1；APPM达到0.8085的Micro-F1，与独立逐任务上界仅差2.7%，合并成本仅156ms且无额外运行时内存开销。在DIVE数据集上验证了框架对三个挑战的有效性。

Conclusion: 该三阶段集成框架有效解决了智能合约漏洞检测中参数高效适配、灾难性遗忘和多适配器合并这三个因果关联的挑战，适用于不断演化的区块链生态。

Abstract: Smart contract vulnerability detection with Large Language Models (LLMs) faces three causally linked challenges. First, new vulnerability categories demand parameter-efficient adaptation, since full retraining is prohibitive for sequentially arriving tasks. Second, training per-task adapters on a shared backbone causes catastrophic forgetting of previously learned vulnerabilities. Third, the resulting multiplicity of adapters must be consolidated into a single model, since task identity is unknown at inference time. Each challenge arises directly from the solution to its predecessor, making an integrated framework essential. We propose a three-stage pipeline in which each stage addresses one challenge and feeds into the next. The adaptation stage uses Frequency-Aware Low-Rank Adaptation (FA-LoRA), which performs adaptation in the Fourier domain with per-frequency importance gates, requiring only 0.4% trainable parameters while outperforming standard LoRA and QLoRA. The continual learning stage applies Forget-Aware Replay (FAR), which uses these frequency gates to estimate per-sample forgetting risk via loss dynamics and prioritizes vulnerable knowledge for rehearsal, achieving an average Micro-F1 of 0.8022 across sequential tasks. The deployment stage employs Anchor-Protected Progressive Merging (APPM), which exploits the asymmetric generalization produced by FAR training to identify the strongest-generalizing adapter as an anchor and consolidates all adapters into a single model via anchor-protected weighted merging with frequency-domain gate competition. APPM achieves a Micro-F1 of 0.8085, within 2.7% of the independent per-task upper bound, at a merge cost of 156 ms and no additional runtime memory. Experiments on DIVE confirm the framework effectively addresses all three challenges for evolving blockchain ecosystems.

</details>


### [89] [Learning Hierarchical Skill Policies with Offline Quality-Diversity Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19684)
*Tanachai Anakewat,Takayuki Osa,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QDOS提出了一种统一的离线到在线强化学习流水线，通过优势加权的质量多样性预训练目标，从离线数据中提取多样且高价值的技能，并采用双数据集重用策略同时用于技能预训练和在线回放缓冲区填充，在结构化操作和非结构运动任务上显著优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两阶段RL策略（先提取低层技能再从数据中训练高层策略）中，低层技能的提取通常依赖无监督学习（如轨迹VAE），其质量高度依赖于数据集本身的质量，导致在低质量数据下技能提取效果不佳，限制了后续任务求解的性能。

Method: QDOS包含两个核心组件：(1) 优势加权质量多样性（Advantage-Weighted Quality-Diversity）预训练目标，根据每条轨迹片段的估计优势来加权技能提取和多样性目标，从而提取多样化且高价值的技能，提升低层策略嵌入技能空间的质量；(2) 双数据集重用策略，离线数据既用于技能预训练，又通过伪标签方式填充在线回放缓冲区，实现离线与在线学习的有效衔接。

Result: 实验表明QDOS在结构化操作任务和非结构化运动任务上均显著优于强基线方法，尤其在具有挑战性的稀疏奖励场景中，证实了该方法能够加速探索并提升最终回报。

Conclusion: 通过优势加权的方式将质量多样性目标引入技能提取过程，并配合双数据集重用策略，QDOS有效提升了离线到在线RL的学习鲁棒性和样本效率，为低质量数据集下的技能学习方法提供了一种可行且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent studies investigate how to leverage pre-collected datasets to improve the policy performance and sample efficiency of RL. One promising approach to achieve this goal is to employ a two-stage strategy: In the first stage, diverse skills are extracted as a low-level policy from a given dataset, and a high-level policy is trained to solve a specific task in the second stage. Typically, extraction of the low-level policy is performed based on unsupervised learning such as trajectory VAE. However, a limitation of this approach is that the quality of the low-level policy highly depends on the quality of the dataset. To address this issue, we introduce QDOS (Quality-Diversity Offline Skill learning), a unified pipeline for robust offline-to-online learning. Our approach incorporates an Advantage-Weighted Quality-Diversity pretraining objective, which weights the skill extraction and diversity objectives by the estimated advantage of each trajectory segment. This approach allows the model to extract diverse and high-value skills. By providing robust and task-relevant skill representations, QDOS significantly improves the quality of the embedded skill space used by the low-level policy. We further integrate this with a dual dataset reuse strategy, where offline data is used both for skill pretraining and for populating the online replay buffer via pseudo-labeling. Experiments demonstrate that QDOS significantly outperforms strong baselines in structured manipulation tasks and unstructured locomotion tasks, confirming its ability to accelerate exploration and improve final returns in challenging sparse-reward domains.

</details>


### [90] [Rethinking the Evaluation and Optimization of LLM-Based Social Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19689)
*Pei Wang,Xu Chen,Ji-Rong Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM社交模拟中人行为保真度评估与优化问题，指出基于准确率的评估和硬标签训练在主观行为场景下不可靠。作者提出主观性系数来刻画任务主观程度，并据此提出SALT（主观性自适应软标签训练）方法，利用语义相近输入的输出池化构造软分布标签，聚合半径随主观性自适应调整。同时构建了SUBJSIM基准（19,300个上下文、193名注释者、100个主观问题），在仅含单次观测的训练数据下验证了该方法优于现有做法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM社交模拟评估和训练依赖于准确率标准：检查模型是否选择了人类观察到的单一响应，并训练模型复现该硬标签。然而人类行为具有主观性，同一人在同一情境下可能有多种合理行为，单一观测只是潜在响应分布的一次抽样，因此准确率评估不可靠、硬标签训练具有误导性。本文旨在解决这一根本性问题。

Method: 首先提出主观性系数（subjectivity coefficient），一种基于熵的量，用于区分客观任务（如编码）与主观任务（如社交模拟）。在此基础上提出SALT：将语义相近输入的观测输出池化，构造软分布标签，聚合半径根据各输入的主观性估计自适应调整；在接近客观的极限情况下邻域收缩，自然退化为标准单标签训练。此外构建SUBJSIM基准数据集，包含19,300个上下文、193名注释者和100个主观问题，支持分布级评估。

Result: 在SUBJSIM上的实验表明，SALT相比现有准确率驱动方法具有明显优势，且训练仅使用单次观测输出、评估针对完整响应分布，验证了在现实（每输入仅一次观测）条件下的可行性。

Conclusion: 准确率导向的评估与硬标签训练不适合主观性强的社交模拟任务；通过主观性系数量化主观程度并以软分布标签自适应训练，可显著提升LLM模拟人类行为的保真度，为社交模拟LLM的评估与优化提供了更可靠的理论框架与基准资源。

Abstract: LLM-based social simulation is a promising complement to traditional methods such as surveys and behavioral experiments. A core question is how to evaluate the fidelity of LLM-simulated human behavior and optimize LLMs toward it. Prevailing practice evaluates by accuracy, checking whether the model selects the single response observed from a human, and trains the LLM to reproduce this hard label. However, human behavior is inherently subjective: the same person in the same situation may reasonably act differently, so an observed response is only one draw from an underlying response distribution, rendering accuracy-based evaluation unreliable and hard-label training misleading. To address these problems, we first introduce the subjectivity coefficient, an entropy-based quantity distinguishing objective tasks such as coding from subjective ones such as social simulation, and use it to systematically analyze how accuracy-based evaluation and hard-label training fail as subjectivity grows. Based on the subjectivity coefficient, we propose Subjectivity-Adaptive soft-Label Training (SALT): it pools observed outputs from semantically nearby inputs into soft distributional labels, with an aggregation radius adapted to the estimated subjectivity of each input; in the near-objective limit the neighborhood shrinks, so SALT naturally falls back to standard single-label training. Moreover, since existing datasets record only single observed responses and cannot support distributional evaluation, we construct SUBJSIM, a benchmark of 19,300 contexts covering 193 annotators and 100 subjective questions. Since real-world data typically provide only a single observation per input, our experiments train models from single observed outputs while evaluating them against the full response distributions, verifying feasibility in realistic settings. Results on SUBJSIM demonstrate the advantages of our method.

</details>


### [91] [Beyond Memory Majority: Latent-Source Reasoning for Multi-Agent Memory Arbitration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19701)
*Chenchen Lin,Wenhao Yuan,Xuehe Wang,Edith Cheuk Han Ngai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CAMA框架，解决多智能体长期系统中因记忆相关性导致的"虚假多数"偏差，通过解耦检索记忆、估计独立证据源数量，并学习序贯证据恢复策略来提升多智能体记忆仲裁的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体记忆方法将检索到的记忆视为独立证据进行投票或加权融合，但这一独立性假设在多智能体场景中常不成立——不同智能体写入的记忆可能继承同一上游来源或共享偏差，导致相关证据被重复计数，形成虚假多数。作者将此失效模式命名为"记忆相关性偏差"（Memory Correlation Bias）。

Method: 提出相关性感知记忆仲裁（CAMA）框架，包含两部分：(1) 联合解耦检索记忆——将检索到的记忆建模为查询条件下的证据组，结合神经依赖推理与基于来源的符号先验，估计有效独立证据源的数量，防止相关记忆形成虚假多数；(2) 序贯恢复策略——由于关键独立证据可能不在初始检索集中，学习一个序贯恢复策略，在最终决策前主动检索替代证据或追踪上游来源，在最小化检索成本的同时恢复足够的独立证据用于可靠仲裁。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，CAMA优于当前最先进的基线方法，能有效抑制由相关记忆诱导的虚假多数。

Conclusion: CAMA框架通过显式建模记忆间的依赖关系并主动恢复缺失的独立证据，有效缓解了多智能体长期系统中的记忆相关性偏差，提升了记忆仲裁的可靠性。

Abstract: Long-term multi-agent systems continuously accumulate the memories produced by different agents. Existing memory methods typically treat retrieved memories as independent evidence and combine them through voting or weighting. However, this independence assumption often fails in multi-agent settings: memories written by different agents may inherit the same upstream source or shared bias, causing correlated evidence to be repeatedly counted and creating a false majority. We term this failure mode \textit{Memory Correlation Bias}. To address the issue, we propose the \textbf{C}orrelation-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{M}emory \textbf{A}rbitration (CAMA) framework that jointly decouples retrieved memories and recovers missing independent evidence. We model the retrieved memories as query-conditioned evidence groups and combine neural dependency inference with provenance-based symbolic priors to estimate the effective number of independent evidence sources, thereby preventing correlated memories from forming a false majority. Since critical independent evidence may be absent from the initial retrieval set, \textsc{CAMA} further learns a sequential recovery policy that actively retrieves alternative evidence or traces upstream sources before making the final decision, aiming to recover sufficient independent evidence for reliable arbitration while minimizing retrieval cost. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of our method over the state-of-the-art baseline methods, suppressing false majorities induced by correlated memories.

</details>


### [92] [SafeBranch: Branch-Pair Safety Alignment for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19729)
*Hyunse Lee,Jiwoo Jeong,Haneul Lee,Kyochul Jang,Youngjae Yu,Woojin Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SafeBranch 是一个针对具身智能体安全对齐的框架，通过将智能体自身的不安全轨迹回滚到致错的安全关键步骤，构建仅在一步上存在差异的分支对来训练智能体，从而在不牺牲任务成功率的前提下实现安全行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视觉语言模型的具身智能体虽能完成任务，但常违反安全约束，而训练安全行为面临两大困难：一是安全与任务成功是不同目标，且安全仅体现在少量关键步骤中；二是传统监督不足——模仿安全轨迹无法解释安全原因，对比任意安全/不安全轨迹会混入无关差异。

Method: SafeBranch 利用环境回滚机制，将智能体自身产生的不安全轨迹回滚到导致违规的安全关键步骤，然后查询智能体生成一个安全替代动作，并将原始动作与替代动作配对，使得两条分支仅在关键步骤上存在差异，从而构建精确的安全对齐训练数据。

Result: 在 IS-Bench、SafetyALFRED 及其包含未见任务和物体的分布外变体上，SafeBranch 能可靠地处理安全问题且不牺牲任务成功率。在未见物体变体上，其安全成功次数约为未训练基线的十倍。

Conclusion: SafeBranch 通过精确定位安全关键差异的分支配对训练，实现了无需部署期评论器（critic）的安全具身智能体，在保持任务性能的同时显著提升安全可靠性，且在分布外场景下表现出较强泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-model-based embodied agents can complete instructed tasks but often violate safety constraints in the process, a problem recently framed as interactive safety. Training such agents to act safely is difficult, since safety and task success are distinct objectives, and safety arises only at a small number of safety-critical steps within a trajectory. Standard supervision is insufficient: imitating safe trajectories teaches behavior without explaining why it is safe, and contrasting arbitrary safe and unsafe trajectories mixes the safety signal with unrelated differences. We propose SafeBranch, a framework that aligns an embodied actor on safety through branch pairs constructed from the actor's own unsafe rollouts via environment rollback. SafeBranch rolls each unsafe rollout back to the safety-critical step that caused the violation, queries the actor for a safe alternative, and pairs the original action with the alternative so that the two branches differ only at that step. The trained actor acts safely at deployment with no critic in the loop. On IS-Bench, SafetyALFRED, and out-of-distribution variants with unseen tasks and objects, it handles safety reliably without sacrificing task success, achieving roughly ten times more safe successes than the untrained baseline on the unseen-object variant.

</details>


### [93] [GenMatch: An End-to-End Generative Matching Framework for Micro-View Order-Dispatching in Ride-Hailing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19751)
*Chuang Liu,Yuxueqing Zhang,Tengfei Lyu,Zirui Yuan,Weiqi Hu,Yanghan Cheng,Ming Wang,Li Ma,Zihao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GenMatch，一个端到端的生成式匹配框架，用于解决网约车平台中微观视角的订单派发问题。该框架通过上下文感知的二分图编码器、业务感知的效用学习器和状态感知的指针解码器，统一了传统的多阶段（预测-估值-匹配）派单流程。在滴滴国际出行的五个城市中进行了离线评估和在线A/B测试，均优于竞争基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统工业界的派单方案采用多阶段范式（模型预测、价值计算、派单匹配），各阶段分别优化不同的中间目标，导致跨阶段目标不一致——单一阶段性能提升并不一定能改善最终的批次级派单结果。因此需要一种端到端的方法来直接优化最终的派单目标。

Method: GenMatch包含三个核心组件：1）Context-Aware Bipartite Encoder，高效地对每个派单批次形成的动态稀疏二分图进行结构化批次级编码；2）Business-Aware Utility Learner，从异构反馈中学习统一的业务效用，替代手工设计的价值函数；3）State-Aware Pointer Decoder，在生成派单分配时跟踪演化的匹配状态，因为每个选中的订单-司机对会改变剩余可行候选集。

Result: 在滴滴国际出行的五个城市的离线评估和在线A/B测试中，GenMatch相对于竞争基线展示了一致的改进，证明了其在工业订单派发中的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: GenMatch作为首个部署于真实生产环境的端到端生成式匹配框架，通过生成式建模将派单问题统一为单一优化目标，解决了跨阶段目标不一致的问题，并在真实工业场景中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Micro-View Order-Dispatching assigns available drivers to passenger orders within each dispatch batch and is critical to the service quality and operational efficiency of ride-hailing platforms. Mainstream industrial solutions follow a multi-stage paradigm of model prediction, value calculation, and dispatch matching. Although dispatch quality is determined by the final batch-level assignment, these stages optimize different intermediate objectives. This cross-stage objective inconsistency means that improving a single stage does not necessarily improve the overall dispatch result. We therefore formulate Micro-View Order-Dispatching as a generative matching problem and propose GenMatch, an end-to-end Generative Matching framework and the first such framework deployed in a real-world production environment. Applying generative modeling to this problem introduces three challenges. First, each dispatch batch forms a dynamic sparse bipartite graph, requiring efficient structured batch-level encoding. Second, replacing the hand-crafted value function requires learning unified business utility from heterogeneous feedback. Third, directly generating an assignment requires tracking the evolving matching state because each selected order-driver pair changes the remaining feasible candidates. GenMatch addresses these challenges with a Context-Aware Bipartite Encoder, a Business-Aware Utility Learner, and a State-Aware Pointer Decoder. Extensive offline evaluations and online A/B tests in five cities across DiDi's international ride-hailing markets show consistent improvements over competitive baselines, confirming the effectiveness and practicality of GenMatch for industrial order-dispatching.

</details>


### [94] [TT-net: Quantum Inspired Tensor Network Denoising in Conditional GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19789)
*Michal A. Sterzel,Marko J. Rančić*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TT-Net，用双切张量列车（Tensor Train）分解替代条件GAN图像去噪中的单切SVD分解，使网络能直接访问跨通道信息。在三种噪声（高斯、运动模糊、椒盐）下，TT-Net在PSNR和SSIM上均优于SVD-Net，且在高斯噪声下优于EigenGAN和Pix2pix基线。训练动力学分析显示其对抗损失饱和但重建质量持续提升，引发对抗组件贡献的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件GAN图像去噪架构中，SVD仅作为单切分解步骤应用于生成器特征图，无法访问跨通道信息。作者希望利用量子物理启发的张量网络方法（具体为张量列车/矩阵乘积态），通过双切分解突破这一限制，验证跨通道信息访问对去噪质量的提升作用。

Method: TT-Net将SVD-Net中的单切SVD去噪块替换为双切张量列车分解（tensor-train decomposition），使分解过程能够同时考虑多个通道间的关联信息。在受控对比实验中，仅改变分解机制这一变量，其他架构保持相同。文中定义了SVD-Net作为基线，并在训练动力学层面对抗损失的变化进行了跟踪分析。

Result: 在三种噪声类型（高斯噪声、运动模糊、椒盐噪声）下，TT-Net在PSNR和SSIM指标上均优于SVD-Net。对高斯噪声，TT-Net同时超越了EigenGAN和Pix2pix（当前最优方法）。训练动力学显示TT-Net的对抗损失项在三种噪声下均趋于停滞饱和状态，比SVD-Net更明显，但重建质量仍持续改善。

Conclusion: 跨通道信息访问确实能提升图像去噪质量，验证了张量列车分解作为一种量子启发工具在深度学习特征过滤中的实用价值。同时，对抗损失饱和而重建质量仍提升的现象表明对抗组件在训练后期可能贡献有限，这是一个识别但未解决的开放问题。

Abstract: Developed as a workhorse for classical simulations of quantum algorithms and quantum many-body systems, Tensor Network methods have entered the scientific mainstream in quantum physics. Among various types of tensor networks, Tensor Trains (commonly know as Matrix Product States in the quantum computing community) have already found applications in machine learning. These methods often rely on a powerful linear algebra tool called the Singular Value Decomposition (SVD). Several conditional GAN architectures for image denoising incorporate SVD as a single-cut decomposition step applied to generator feature maps. In this work we introduce TT-Net, which replaces the per-channel SVD denoising block with a two-cut tensor-train decomposition capable of accessing cross-channel information directly, a capability absent from contemporary alternatives. In a controlled comparison differing only in this decomposition mechanism, TT-Net outperforms SVD-Net on PSNR and SSIM across all three noise types tested (Gaussian, motion blur, and salt-and-pepper), supporting the hypothesis that cross-channel access improves denoising quality. Training-dynamics analysis further shows that TT-Net's adversarial loss term consistently saturates to a stagnant state across all three noise types, more so than SVD-Net's, while reconstruction quality continues to improve regardless, raising an open question about the adversarial component's contribution that this work identifies but does not resolve. Furthermore, for Gaussian noise our method outperforms both the EigenGAN and the state of the art Pix2pix method which does not assume any linear algebra decompositions and does not retain any linear algebra information. Our manuscript shows how quantum inspired tools can be used as practical real world feature filters for deep learning applications.

</details>


### [95] [LLMs as Acquisition Policies for Finite-Pool Materials Optimization: A Controlled Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19790)
*Dino-Rober Demir,Florian Le Bronnec,Rio Yokota*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了开源大语言模型（LLMs）是否能作为独立采集策略用于有限池材料优化任务。通过五个LLM在四个回溯性任务上的评估，发现LLM策略通常比随机选择更快达到全局最优，但相对于高斯过程方法表现不一，总体显示出潜力但可靠性受任务和呈现方式影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料发现需要在大型候选空间中搜索，而实验或计算评估成本高昂。主动学习通过代理模型选择下一个评估候选来应对这一挑战，本文探讨开源LLM能否直接作为采集策略，无需任务特定训练即可提供有效的采集信号。

Method: 在四个回溯性有限池材料优化任务中，评估了五种开源LLM作为采集策略的性能，测试了不同的候选呈现策略，并将其与随机选择及传统高斯过程方法（如常规采集函数）进行对比。

Result: LLM策略普遍比随机选择更快达到全局最优，表明其无需训练即可提供有用的采集信号。但与高斯过程方法相比表现混杂：常规采集在多数任务上更优，LLM在部分设置下能匹配或超越。性能在任务、模型、初始化和候选呈现方式间差异显著，没有一种LLM方法在所有任务中表现最佳。

Conclusion: 开源LLM作为采集策略在有限池材料搜索中展现出潜力，但其可靠性对任务特性以及候选和科学上下文的呈现方式高度敏感，尚需进一步研究以提升其鲁棒性。

Abstract: Discovering materials with desirable properties often requires searching large candidate spaces while experimental or computational evaluations remain costly. Active learning addresses this challenge by using previous observations to select which candidate to evaluate next, typically through probabilistic surrogate models. We investigate whether open-weight large language models (LLMs) can serve as standalone acquisition policies in this setting. We evaluate five LLMs across four retrospective finite-pool materials optimization tasks under different candidate-presentation strategies and compare them with random selection and conventional Gaussian-process methods. LLM policies generally reach the global optimum in fewer iterations than random selection, indicating that they provide a useful acquisition signal without task-specific training. Their performance relative to Gaussian-process methods is mixed: conventional acquisition performs better on most tasks, while LLMs match or outperform it in some settings. Performance varies substantially across tasks, models, initializations, and candidate presentations, with no LLM approach performing best across all tasks. Overall, open-weight LLMs show potential as acquisition policies for finite-pool materials search, although their reliability remains sensitive to the task and to how candidates and scientific context are presented.

</details>


### [96] [Towards general embodied intelligence: integrating large language models, knowledge bases, and reasoning capabilities to build the next generation of AI agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19794)
*Fujiang Yuan,Xia Huang,Lusheng Wang,Jun Ding,Zhen Tian,Yuxin Wang,Shaojie Gu,Yuki Funabora,Yanhong Peng,Zebing Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了大语言模型、结构化知识库与推理能力三者融合的发展脉络，提出了一个概念性框架统一阐述LLMs、KBs、RA与具身智能之间的协同关系，并识别出通往通用具身智能的五个关键挑战，为构建自适应多模态智能体提供了研究路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs、知识库和推理能力的快速发展，如何将它们有效整合以推动通用具身智能（GEI）的实现成为核心问题。现有研究大多孤立地处理这些维度，缺乏统一的视角来理解它们之间的协同关系，因此需要一篇系统性综述来梳理进展并明确未来方向。

Method: 论文采用文献综述的方法，系统分析了LLM的架构、预训练方法和推理机制，探讨了其与外部知识源及结构化推理框架的交互方式；同时考察了具身智能范式，即智能体在物理环境中学习与行动的机制；最终提出一个概念性框架，整合LLMs、KBs、RA和具身化四个维度作为感知-推理-行动的指导模型（而非工程实现架构）。

Result: 论文提出了一个概念性框架来阐明LLMs、KBs、RA与具身化之间的协同关系，并识别出通往GEI的五大关键挑战：高效LLM部署、闭环知识整合、混合符号-神经推理、感知-行动接地以及持续学习。

Conclusion: 该综述为开发能够在复杂动态环境中运行的自适应多模态智能体提供了全面的研究路线图，指出LLM-知识-推理-具身四维融合是实现通用具身智能的核心路径，未来需要在五大挑战方向上集中突破。

Abstract: The convergence of large language models (LLMs), structured knowledge bases (KBs), and reasoning ability (RA) presents a promising trajectory toward general embodied intelligence (GEI). This paper reviews the evolution of LLM-centered intelligent systems, emphasising their integration with knowledge representation, logical reasoning, and physical embodiment. We analyse LLM architectures, pre-training methods, and inference mechanisms, along with their interaction with external knowledge sources and structured reasoning frameworks. Furthermore, we examine embodied intelligence (EI) paradigms wherein agents learn and act in physical environments. To synthesise these dimensions, we present a conceptual framework that illustrates the synergy among LLMs, KBs, RA, and embodiment, serving as a guiding model for perception, reasoning, and action rather than an implemented engineering architecture. To advance toward GEI, we identify five key challenges: efficient LLM deployment, closed-loop knowledge integration, hybrid symbolic-neural reasoning, perception-action grounding, and continual learning. This survey provides a comprehensive roadmap for developing adaptive, multimodal agents capable of operating in complex, dynamic settings.

</details>


### [97] [ADAPT: Physics-Aware Diffusion-based World Models for Adaptive Predictive Transferable HVAC Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19804)
*Xu Yang,Kailai Sun,Dianyu Zhong,Qianchuan Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ADAPT，一种物理感知的条件扩散室内环境世界模型，用于HVAC控制系统。通过短时程热基线捕捉建筑隐式热惯性，利用扩散生成模型的鲁棒性，并结合可学习的多区域热平衡正则化器约束生成轨迹满足可迁移的建筑热力学，无需已知建筑几何或人工标定热参数。在SemibuildingSim和Sinergym上的实验表明，ADAPT在IID控制下降低7.3%能耗和30.2%居住不舒适度，在OOD场景下仅出现边际性能退化，远超现有方法的迁移鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑占全球约三分之一的能源消耗和CO2排放，优化室内气候系统对城市气候缓解和UN SDG 11/13至关重要。然而，现有方法受限于室内延迟热力学响应、部分可观测性、隐式热惯性、占用动态预测和累积预测误差等问题，尤其在分布外环境下性能严重退化。实践中，高成本和高隐私负担限制密集室内传感，只能获取单一运行状态下的有限数据，却期望控制器能在未见季节和气候区域泛化。因此需要一种能在数据稀缺和OOD条件下可靠泛化的HVAC控制方法。

Method: ADAPT包含三个核心组件：(1) 短时程持有动作热基线预测，用于捕捉建筑的隐式热惯性；(2) 扩散模型骨干网络，利用生成模型的鲁棒性进行室内环境预测；(3) 可学习的多区域热平衡正则化器，约束生成轨迹满足可迁移的建筑热力学，无需已知建筑几何或手动校准热参数。此外设计了面向下游强化学习的信用分配机制，将预测与RL控制器有效衔接。

Result: 在SemibuildingSim和Sinergym两个模拟平台上实验：IID控制下，ADAPT相比最先进基线降低7.3%的HVAC能耗和30.2%的居住不舒适度；在覆盖未见季节和气候区域的OOD控制场景下，ADAPT保持了鲁棒性能，仅相对其IID性能出现边际退化，迁移鲁棒性显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: ADAPT通过物理感知的条件扩散模型有效解决了HVAC控制中隐式热惯性建模、分布外泛化和数据稀缺三大难题，证明了将生成模型与可迁移热力学先验结合能够在复杂、部分可观测的建筑环境中实现高效且鲁棒的HVAC智能控制，为建筑节能和城市气候缓解提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Buildings account for roughly one-third of global energy consumption and CO$_2$ emissions. Optimizing indoor climate systems plays a critical role for urban climate mitigation aligned with UN Sustainable Development Goals 11 and 13. However, indoor delayed thermodynamic responses and partial observability severely hinder existing methods, which are primarily limited by implicit thermal inertia, occupancy dynamic prediction, and cumulative prediction errors, especially for out-of-distribution environments. In practice, these challenges are further exacerbated by the high cost and privacy burden of dense indoor sensing, forcing operators to collect only limited data in a single operating regime while expecting controllers to generalize reliably across unseen seasons and climate regions. To address this problem, we propose ADAPT, a physics-aware conditional diffusion indoor environmental world model for HVAC control. The model predicts a short-horizon held-action thermal baseline to capture the latent thermal inertia of the buildings. The diffusion backbone utilizes the robustness of generative models, while a learnable multi-zone heat-balance regularizer constrains generated trajectories to satisfy transferable building thermodynamics without requiring known building geometry or manually calibrated thermal parameters. A credit assignment is then design for the downstream reinforcement learning. Extensive experiments on SemibuildingSim and Sinergym demonstrate that ADAPT reduces HVAC energy consumption by 7.3\% and occupant discomfort by 30.2\% compared with state-of-the-art baselines under IID control. Under OOD control scenarios spanning unseen seasons and climate regions, ADAPT maintains robust performance with only marginal degradation relative to its IID performance, substantially outperforming existing methods in transfer robustness.

</details>


### [98] [When Saying No Makes Better Videos: Designing Dual Gatekeeping for Pedagogically Grounded AI Content Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19812)
*Yearim Kim,Njun Baek,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个视频创作流水线，通过两层结构化"拒绝"机制（教育者迭代重塑AI脚本+自动化指标检测）来防止采用美观但教学上有缺陷的AI内容，实验表明两者能独立提升相同的教学维度，证明审慎抵抗与生成式AI是合作伙伴而非对立面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI在教育内容创作中的普及，出现了"美观但教学上有缺陷"的AI内容风险。作者旨在防止这类表面精致但教学法上有缺陷的内容被采用，因此研究如何通过结构化拒绝机制来确保AI产出符合严格的教育标准。

Method: 提出一个包含两层结构化拒绝的视频创作流水线：第一层赋予教育者基于多媒体学习理论迭代重塑AI脚本的能力；第二层采用自动化指标来标记教学连贯性和叙事-视觉同步方面的违规。评估结合了23名教育者在3个主题上的研究和7个主题上的自动化指标（基于既定科学与哲学课程）。

Result: 两层机制都能独立提升相同的教学维度；两层协同作用表明，将AI输出推迟直至达到严格标准的"原则性抵抗"行为成为更高质量内容的催化剂。

Conclusion: 审慎抵抗（延迟采用AI输出直到其满足严格标准）与生成式AI并非对立关系，而是互补的合作伙伴关系，两者结合可以提升教育视频内容的质量。

Abstract: To prevent the adoption of aesthetically polished but pedagogically flawed AI content, we study a video authoring pipeline featuring two layers of structured refusal. The first layer empowers educators to iteratively reshape AI scripts based on multimedia learning theory, while the second employs automated metrics to flag violations in instructional coherence and narrative-visual synchronization. While neither layer is exhaustive, their synergy ensures that principled resistance--the act of deferring AI output until it meets rigorous standards--becomes a catalyst for higher quality. Evaluation combining a study with 23 educators across 3 topics and automated metrics across 7 topics drawn from established science and philosophy curricula shows that both layers independently improve the same instructional dimensions, suggesting that thoughtful resistance and generative AI are not opposites but partners.

</details>


### [99] [Causal Reasoning with Bipartite Graphical Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19831)
*Joris M. Mooij*

Main category: cs.AI
